// GB3\n949: \t\t}\n950: \t}\n951: \tif (breakTypeA === GraphemeBreakType.Control || breakTypeA === GraphemeBreakType.CR || breakTypeA === GraphemeBreakType.LF) {\n952: \t\treturn true; // GB4\n953: \t}\n954: \tif (breakTypeB === GraphemeBreakType.Control || breakTypeB === GraphemeBreakType.CR || breakTypeB === GraphemeBreakType.LF) {\n955: \t\treturn true; The negation of this—when we should return false—is if otherRange starts before range or ends after range. The column boundary checks apply when the ranges share start or end lines.
